\## left/right (5)

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="\begin{array}{l}{P_{a}(1)=P_{b}(1)=P_{c}(1)=P_{a^{\prime}}(1)=P_{b^{\prime}}(1)=P_{c^{\prime}}(1)=1} \\ {P_{a}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{b}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{c}\left(\omega^{n}\right)=P_{a^{\prime}}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{b^{\prime}}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{c^{\prime}}\left(\omega^{n}\right)}\end{array}" data-display="yes" data-evaluate="no">$$\begin{array}{l}{P_{a}(1)=P_{b}(1)=P_{c}(1)=P_{a^{\prime}}(1)=P_{b^{\prime}}(1)=P_{c^{\prime}}(1)=1} \\ {P_{a}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{b}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{c}\left(\omega^{n}\right)=P_{a^{\prime}}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{b^{\prime}}\left(\omega^{n}\right) P_{c^{\prime}}\left(\omega^{n}\right)}\end{array}$$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="\begin{array}{l}{P_{a}(\omega x)-P_{a}(x)\left(v_{1}+x+v_{2} a(x)\right) =Z(x) H_{1}(x)} \\ {P_{a^{\prime}}(\omega x)-P_{a^{\prime}}(x)\left(v_{1}+\sigma_{a}(x)+v_{2} a(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{2}(x)} \\ {P_{b}(\omega x)-P_{b}(x)\left(v_{1}+g x+v_{2} b(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{3}(x)} \\ {P_{b^{\prime}}(\omega x)-P_{b^{\prime}}(x)\left(v_{1}+\sigma_{b}(x)+v_{2} b(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{4}(x)} \\ {P_{c}(\omega x)-P_{c}(x)\left(v_{1}+g^{2} x+v_{2} c(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{5}(x)} \\ {P_{c^{\prime}}(\omega x)-P_{c^{\prime}}(x)\left(v_{1}+\sigma_{c}(x)+v_{2} c(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{6}(x)}\end{array}" data-display="yes" data-evaluate="no">$$\begin{array}{l}{P_{a}(\omega x)-P_{a}(x)\left(v_{1}+x+v_{2} a(x)\right) =Z(x) H_{1}(x)} \\ {P_{a^{\prime}}(\omega x)-P_{a^{\prime}}(x)\left(v_{1}+\sigma_{a}(x)+v_{2} a(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{2}(x)} \\ {P_{b}(\omega x)-P_{b}(x)\left(v_{1}+g x+v_{2} b(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{3}(x)} \\ {P_{b^{\prime}}(\omega x)-P_{b^{\prime}}(x)\left(v_{1}+\sigma_{b}(x)+v_{2} b(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{4}(x)} \\ {P_{c}(\omega x)-P_{c}(x)\left(v_{1}+g^{2} x+v_{2} c(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{5}(x)} \\ {P_{c^{\prime}}(\omega x)-P_{c^{\prime}}(x)\left(v_{1}+\sigma_{c}(x)+v_{2} c(x)\right)=Z(x) H_{6}(x)}\end{array}$$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="&lt;ct_1, k> = 2e_1 + m_1" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$\lt ct_1, k> = 2e_1 + m_1$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (2e_1 + m_1) * (2e_2 + m_2)" data-display="yes" data-evaluate="no">$$= (2e_1 + m_1) * (2e_2 + m_2)$$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (A * r_f + m * G + e_1) + (A * s + e_2) - (A * r_f + A * s)" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (A * r_f + m * G + e_1) + (A * s + e_2) - (A * r_f + A * s)$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (A_{prefix}\ |\ G - A_{prefix} * R) * {R \atop I}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (A_{prefix}\ |\ G - A_{prefix} * R) * {R \atop I}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (m \otimes s) * M + e_g" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (m \otimes s) * M + e_g$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (m \otimes s) * P_L * K_G + e_L * K_G" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (m \otimes s) * P_L * K_G + e_L * K_G$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (m_1 * k + e_1) * CT_2" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (m_1 * k + e_1) * CT_2$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (m_L \otimes s_L) * P_L * K_f + e_L * K_f" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (m_L \otimes s_L) * P_L * K_f + e_L * K_f$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (m_L \otimes s_L) * u_1 \otimes B_{full} - (m_L \otimes s_L) * (I \otimes G) + e_f" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (m_L \otimes s_L) * u_1 \otimes B_{full} - (m_L \otimes s_L) * (I \otimes G) + e_f$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (s * (x_R * G + B_R) + e_R) * R_{-L}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (s * (x_R * G + B_R) + e_R) * R_{-L}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= (s * C_1) * C_2" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= (s * C_1) * C_2$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= 1 + x_0 + x_2 + x_2x_1 + x_3 + x_2x_3 + x_0x_2x_3 + x_1x_2x_3 + x_0x_1x_2x_3" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= 1 + x_0 + x_2 + x_2x_1 + x_3 + x_2x_3 + x_0x_2x_3 + x_1x_2x_3 + x_0x_1x_2x_3$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= 2(e_1m_2 + e_2m_1 + 2e_1e_2) + m_1m_2" data-display="yes" data-evaluate="no">$$= 2(e_1m_2 + e_2m_1 + 2e_1e_2) + m_1m_2$$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= &lt;c_1, k> * &lt;c_2, k>" data-display="yes" data-evaluate="no">$$= \lt c_1, k> * \lt c_2, k>$$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= A_{prefix} * R + (G - A_{prefix} * R) * I" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= A_{prefix} * R + (G - A_{prefix} * R) * I$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= A_{prefix} * R + G - A_{prefix} * R" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= A_{prefix} * R + G - A_{prefix} * R$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= \texttt{1100} + \texttt{1010} * x_2 + \texttt{1000} * x_3 + \texttt{1111} * x_2x_3" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= \texttt{1100} + \texttt{1010} * x_2 + \texttt{1000} * x_3 + \texttt{1111} * x_2x_3$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= \texttt{11} + \texttt{10} * x_2 + \texttt{10} * x_2x_1 + \texttt{10} * x_3 + \texttt{11} * x_2x_3 + \texttt{11} * x_1x_2x_3" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= \texttt{11} + \texttt{10} * x_2 + \texttt{10} * x_2x_1 + \texttt{10} * x_3 + \texttt{11} * x_2x_3 + \texttt{11} * x_1x_2x_3$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= m * G + e_1 - e_2" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= m * G + e_1 - e_2$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= m_1 * k * CT_2 + e_1 * CT_2" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= m_1 * k * CT_2 + e_1 * CT_2$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= m_1 * m_2 * k + m_1 * e_2 + e_1 * CT_2" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= m_1 * m_2 * k + m_1 * e_2 + e_1 * CT_2$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= q_0 * P_0 * K_{\{1,b_1\}} + e_0 * K_{\{1,b_1\}}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= q_0 * P_0 * K_{\{1,b_1\}} + e_0 * K_{\{1,b_1\}}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * P_1 * K_{\{2,b_2\}} + e_1 * K_{\{2,b_2\}}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * P_1 * K_{\{2,b_2\}} + e_1 * K_{\{2,b_2\}}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * P_1 + e_0 * K_{\{1,b_1\}} + q_0 * E_{\{1,b_1\}}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * P_1 + e_0 * K_{\{1,b_1\}} + q_0 * E_{\{1,b_1\}}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * P_1 + e_1" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * P_1 + e_1$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * Q_{\{2,b_2\}} * P_2 + e_1 * K_{\{2,b_2\}} + q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * E_{\{2,b_2\}}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * Q_{\{2,b_2\}} * P_2 + e_1 * K_{\{2,b_2\}} + q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * E_{\{2,b_2\}}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * Q_{{2,b_2}} * P_2 + e_2" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= q_0 * Q_{\{1,b_1\}} * Q_{{2,b_2}} * P_2 + e_2$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= s * (-x_R * B_L + B_R * R_{-L}) + e_R * R_{-L}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= s * (-x_R * B_L + B_R * R_{-L}) + e_R * R_{-L}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= s * (B_{\{out,i,b\}} - (tY)_{\{i,b\}} * G) + e_{\{out,i,b\}}" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= s * (B_{\{out,i,b\}} - (tY)_{\{i,b\}} * G) + e_{\{out,i,b\}}$</span>

<span data-type="inlineMath" data-latex="= s * (x_L * x_R * G + x_R * B_L) + x_R * e_L" data-display="no" data-evaluate="no">$= s * (x_L * x_R * G + x_R * B_L) + x_R * e_L$</span>
